wet_grn_transfer_skarsnik = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      U38 = {
        has_character = WET_U38_leader
      }
    }
    U38 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_leader = {
            character = WET_U38_leader
            ruling_only = yes
          }
        }
        add_country_leader_role = {
          character = WET_U38_commander_marshal
          promote_leader = yes
          country_leader = {
            ideology = wet_waaagh_gork_n_mork_religion
            expire = "2902.1.31.1"
            traits = {
              
            }
          }
        }
        country_event = {
          id = wet_grn.2
        }
      }
      remove_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_U38_leader
        ideology = wet_waaagh_gork_n_mork_religion
      }
      WET_U38_leader = {
        set_nationality = G24
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_grn_absorb = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_grn_opening_done = yes
      FROM = {
        wet_grn_absorbable = yes
      }
    }
    clear_array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
    FROM = {
      every_owned_state = {
        limit = {
          impassable = no
        }
        G24 = {
          add_to_array = {
            wet_grn_absorbed_states = PREV
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          is_in_array = {
            wet_grn_absorbed_polities = FROM
          }
        }
      }
      add_to_array = {
        wet_grn_absorbed_polities = FROM
      }
      add_to_variable = {
        wet_grn_absorptions = 1
      }
    }
    FROM = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_character = WET_U38_leader
        }
        remove_country_leader_role = {
          character = WET_U38_leader
          ideology = wet_waaagh_gork_n_mork_religion
        }
        WET_U38_leader = {
          set_nationality = G24
        }
      }
    }
    annex_country = {
      target = FROM
      transfer_troops = yes
    }
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
      if = {
        limit = {
          is_owned_by = G24
        }
        add_core_of = G24
        set_resistance = 0
        set_compliance = 100
      }
    }
    clear_array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
    country_event = {
      id = wet_grn.10
    }
  }
}
wet_grn_assess_factions = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      is_ai = yes
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        NOT = {
          tag = G24
        }
        OR = {
          is_faction_leader = yes
          is_neighbor_of = G24
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_grn_ai_ready
      set_variable = {
        wet_grn_defending_strength = deployed_army_manpower_k
      }
      every_other_country = {
        limit = {
          exists = yes
          OR = {
            is_in_faction_with = PREV
            is_subject_of = PREV
            has_guaranteed = PREV
          }
        }
        PREV = {
          add_to_variable = {
            wet_grn_defending_strength = PREV.deployed_army_manpower_k
          }
        }
      }
      multiply_variable = {
        wet_grn_defending_strength = 1.25
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_grn_defending_strength
            <
            G24.deployed_army_manpower_k
          }
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_grn_ai_ready
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_grn_war_another_faction = {
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_grn_bound_F06
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_grn_war_legal = yes
        wet_grn_unused_faction = yes
        OR = {
          G24 = {
            is_ai = no
          }
          G24 = {
            has_war = no
          }
          has_war_with = G24
        }
      }
      G24 = {
        if = {
          limit = {
            NOT = {
              has_war_with = PREV
            }
          }
          create_wargoal = {
            type = wet_grn_final_war
            target = PREV
            expire = 180
          }
          declare_war_on = {
            target = PREV
            type = wet_grn_final_war
          }
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_war_with = G24
        }
        every_country = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
        set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
        every_other_country = {
          limit = {
            is_in_faction_with = PREV
          }
          every_country = {
            limit = {
              has_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
            }
            clr_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F06
        }
        capital_scope = {
          G24 = {
            clear_array = wet_grn_capital_F06
            add_to_array = {
              wet_grn_capital_F06 = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        G24 = {
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_F06
          set_variable = {
            wet_grn_days_F06 = 180
          }
          country_event = {
            id = wet_grn.50
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  wet_grn_refresh_efforts = yes
}

  }

wet_grn_spend_U05_arms_factory = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {

        >
        0
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        check_variable = {
          wet_grn_claim_U05_arms_factory
          >
          0
        }
        54 = {
          is_owned_by = G24
          is_fully_controlled_by = G24
          impassable = no
          NOT = {
            OR = {
              arms_factory
              >
              4

            building = arms_factory
            size
            >
            0
            include_locked = yes

          type = arms_factory
          level = 1
          instant_build = yes

          set_variable = {
            wet_grn_claim_U05_arms_factory = 0

wet_grn_refresh_programmes = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_grn_opening_done = yes
      NOT = {
        has_dynamic_modifier = wet_grn_programme_host
      }
    }
    add_dynamic_modifier = {
      modifier = wet_grn_programme_host
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_leader = {
        character = WET_G24_leader
        ruling_only = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_dynamic_modifier = wet_grn_programme_G
      }
    }
    add_dynamic_modifier = {
      modifier = wet_grn_programme_G
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_leader = {
        character = WET_U38_leader
        ruling_only = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_dynamic_modifier = wet_grn_programme_K
      }
    }
    add_dynamic_modifier = {
      modifier = wet_grn_programme_K
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_leader = {
        character = WET_GRN_JointOffice
        ruling_only = yes
      }
      has_character = WET_G24_leader
      has_character = WET_U38_leader
      NOT = {
        has_dynamic_modifier = wet_grn_programme_T
      }
    }
    add_dynamic_modifier = {
      modifier = wet_grn_programme_T
    }
  }
}
wet_grn_succession = {

      OR = {

wet_focus_actions_black_crag = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_grn_effort_W09_focus_pending
    }
    random_other_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        has_war_with = G24
        tag = Z01
      }
      save_event_target_as = wet_focus_recipient
      G24 = {
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_completed_focus = WET_GRN_W09
            event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
              exists = yes
              has_war_with = G24
              tag = Z01

wet_grn_diplomatic_absorb_complete = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_grn_diplomatic_absorb = yes
    }
    save_event_target_as = wet_grn_diplomatic_target
    ROOT = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          wet_grn_opening_done = yes
          event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target = {
            wet_grn_absorbable = yes
          }
        }
        clear_array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
        event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target = {
          every_owned_state = {
            limit = {
              impassable = no
            }
            G24 = {
              add_to_array = {
                wet_grn_absorbed_states = PREV
              }
            }
          }
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            NOT = {
              is_in_array = {
                wet_grn_absorbed_polities = event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target
              }
            }
          }
          add_to_array = {
            wet_grn_absorbed_polities = event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target
          }
          add_to_variable = {
            wet_grn_absorptions = 1
          }
        }
        event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target = {
          if = {
            limit = {
              has_character = WET_U38_leader
            }
            remove_country_leader_role = {
              character = WET_U38_leader
              ideology = wet_waaagh_gork_n_mork_religion
            }
            WET_U38_leader = {
              set_nationality = G24
            }
          }
        }
        annex_country = {
          target = event_target:wet_grn_diplomatic_target
          transfer_troops = yes
        }
        for_each_scope_loop = {
          array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
          if = {
            limit = {
              is_owned_by = G24
            }
            add_core_of = G24
            set_resistance = 0
            set_compliance = 100
          }
        }
        clear_array = wet_grn_absorbed_states
        country_event = {
          id = wet_grn.10
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_grn_award_E04 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_E04
      }
      wet_grn_opening_done = yes
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_E04
    set_country_flag = wet_grn_armed_E04
    set_country_flag = wet_grn_effort_E04_focus_pending
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_grn_effort_E04_focus_pending
      }
      random_other_country = {
        limit = {
          exists = yes
          has_war_with = G24
          tag = B17
        }
        save_event_target_as = wet_focus_recipient
        G24 = {
          if = {
            limit = {
              has_completed_focus = WET_GRN_E04
              event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
                exists = yes
                has_war_with = G24
                tag = B17
              }
              NOT = {
                has_country_flag = wet_grn_used_E04
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
                  exists = yes
                  has_war_with = G24
                  tag = B17
                }
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_grn_used_E04
                }
              }
              set_country_flag = wet_grn_used_E04
              set_variable = {
                wet_grn_days_E04 = 120
              }
              clear_array = wet_grn_bound_E04
              add_to_array = {
                wet_grn_bound_E04 = event_target:wet_focus_recipient
              }
              wet_grn_refresh_efforts = yes
            }
            clr_country_flag = wet_grn_effort_E04_focus_pending
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_grn_award_F01 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_F01
      }
      wet_grn_opening_done = yes
      NOT = {
        OR = {
          threat
          <
          1
        }
      }
      any_of_scopes = {
        array = wet_grn_bound_F01
        wet_grn_faction_F01 = yes
        wet_grn_war_legal = yes
        wet_grn_unused_faction = yes
        OR = {
          G24 = {
            is_ai = no
          }
          G24 = {
            has_war = no
          }
          has_war_with = G24
        }
      }
    }
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_grn_bound_F01
      if = {
        limit = {
          wet_grn_faction_F01 = yes
          wet_grn_war_legal = yes
          wet_grn_unused_faction = yes
          OR = {
            G24 = {
              is_ai = no
            }
            G24 = {
              has_war = no
            }
            has_war_with = G24
          }
        }
        G24 = {
          if = {
            limit = {
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = PREV
              }
            }
            create_wargoal = {
              type = wet_grn_final_war
              target = PREV
              expire = 180
            }
            declare_war_on = {
              target = PREV
              type = wet_grn_final_war
            }
          }
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_war_with = G24
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F01
          every_other_country = {
            limit = {
              is_in_faction_with = PREV
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F01
          }
          capital_scope = {
            G24 = {
              clear_array = wet_grn_capital_F01
              add_to_array = {
                wet_grn_capital_F01 = PREV
              }
            }
          }
          G24 = {
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_F01
            set_variable = {
              wet_grn_days_F01 = 180
            }
            country_event = {
              id = wet_grn.50
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_grn_refresh_efforts = yes
  }
}

            limit = {
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = PREV
              }
            }
            create_wargoal = {
              type = wet_grn_final_war
              target = PREV
              expire = 180
            }
            declare_war_on = {
              target = PREV
              type = wet_grn_final_war
            }
          }
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_war_with = G24
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
          set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F04
          every_other_country = {
            limit = {
              is_in_faction_with = PREV
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_final_consumed
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_bloc_F04
          }
          capital_scope = {
            G24 = {
              clear_array = wet_grn_capital_F04
              add_to_array = {
                wet_grn_capital_F04 = PREV
              }
            }
          }
          G24 = {
            set_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_F04
            set_variable = {
              wet_grn_days_F04 = 180
            }
            country_event = {
              id = wet_grn.50
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_grn_refresh_efforts = yes
  }
}
wet_grn_award_F05 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_grn_reward_F05
      }
      wet_grn_opening_done = yes
      NOT = {
        OR = {
          threat
          <
          1
        }
      }
      any_of_scopes = {
        array = wet_grn_bound_F05
        wet_grn_faction_F05 = yes
        wet_grn_war_legal = yes
        wet_grn_unused_faction = yes

          G24 = {
            is_ai = no

        }
